通道及认证刷新链，补充离线契约与回归。原始源码、既有二进制证据、\n+Django 用户库、旧网关库及线上资源均保留；没有导入旧数据。\n+\n+`Config::from_env` 新增 `GATEWAY_UPSTREAM_MODE`：\n+\n+- 缺省或 `offline`：继续仅接受数字 HTTP 回环地址；旧测试无需 CDN 配置。\n+- `configured`：Django、聊天、CDN 必须显式配置；可选 CF 服务单独配置。\n+  接受 HTTP/HTTPS 服务源（含端口），拒绝 URL 凭据、路径前缀、查询串、片段和其他协议。\n+  不回退到默认公网目标，不关闭 TLS 证书校验，不跟随上游重定向。\n+- `CHATGPT_CDN_BASE_URL` 存入 `Config.cdn_upstream`，与 `django`、`upstream` 分离。\n+  **它现已接入下述受限 JS/CSS 路由；不代表完整静态资源或聊天页面可用。**\n+- 请求不能覆盖配置目标。已开放路由仍使用服务端固定源；未知业务路径仍失败。\n+  原有模型、配额、MCP/Skills 策略代码、授权版本检查没有删除或放宽。\n+- 提供 `.env.configured.example`，仅作候选配置说明；没有改动现有部署 compose。\n+\n+## 已检查的契约和证据等级\n+\n+“源码”只表明本地实现；“旧观测”指已有离线模拟上游对原版的观测，不是本次真实环境验收。\n+\n+| 能力/请求 | 请求、ID、返回/错误 | 证据与结论 |\n+|---|---|---|\n+| 登录 | 服务间 `POST /api/login`；`user_name`、`authorization`、上游 token 与已有策略字段；返回 `login_url` | `src/server.rs::login`；`tests/mirror_auth.rs` 合成回归。管理员服务密钥不等于操作者身份 |\n+| 登录交接 | `GET /api/not-login?user_gateway_token=...`；轮换 新候选仅允许 `/assets/` 下或受限 `/cdn/` 映射的安全 ASCII JS/CSS 路径，GET/HEAD；不接受路径穿越、编码路径或任意 URL。缺 CDN/未证实路径503，其他方法405，重定向及HTML/错误MIME502；上游错误状态保留但不泄出错误正文。\n+- 原版登录以及每次 `/api/auth/session`（含重复、`refresh_account=1`）都依次请求 `accounts/check/v4-2023-04-27` 与 `me`。合成 `accounts.default.account.plan_type=plus` 产生 planType=plus；accounts 500时仍请求me并显示free；me 401时返回空对象。**这些是合成输入下的实测解析行为，不是对全部真实响应结构的推定；候选已实现会话刷新，初次登录仍只调用me。**\n+- 启动CF预热可能与首个案例标签交叠；不把标签下出现的预热记录推断为匿名首页的业务调用。\n+\n+## 认证刷新与独立权限模块暂存\n+\n+- `auth_session` → `proxy::refresh_auth_session`：按当前 Isolated ACL v1. Not wired into the gateway, its login, or its database.\n+//! Trust boundary: only a verified Django response and a server-side successful\n+//! creation adapter may construct the inputs. This module performs no network I/O.\n+\n+use std::collections::BTreeSet;\n+use std::fmt;\n+\n+use rusqlite::{params, Connection, OptionalExtension, TransactionBehavior};\n+use serde_json::Value;\n+\n+#[derive(Debug)]\n+pub enum AclError {\n+    Unauthorized,\n+    Forbidden,\n+    UnknownResource,\n+    InvalidInput,\n+    InvalidOperation,\n+    CrossAccount,\n+    AlreadyRegistered,\n+    Sqlite(rusqlite::Error),\n+}\n+\n+impl fmt::Display for AclError {\n+    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n+        write!(f, \"{self:?}\")\n+    }\n+}\n+impl std::error::Error for AclError {}\n+impl From<rusqlite::Error> for AclError {\n+    fn from(error: rusqlite::Error) -> Self {\n+        Self::Sqlite(error)\n+    }\n+}\n+pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, AclError>;\n+\n+#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]\n+pub struct Identity {\n+    user_id: String,\n+    is_admin: bool,\n+    authorization_version: String,\n+}\n+\n+impl Identity {\n+    /// `details` MUST come from the fixed trusted Django authority, never JSON\n+    /// submitted by the browser or possession of the service management key.\n+    pub fn from_authority(details: &Value, expected_subject: &str, now: i64) -> Result<Self> {\n+        let user_id = details[\"user_id\"].as_str().ok_or(AclError::Unauthorized)?;\n+        let version = details[\"version\"].as_str().ok_or(AclError::Unauthorized)?;\n+        let is_admin = details[\"is_admin\"]\n+            .as_bool()\n+            .ok_or(AclError::Unauthorized)?;\n+        if details[\"active\"] != true\n+            || details[\"principal_kind\"] != \"user\"\n+            || expected_subject.is_empty()\n+            || details[\"subject\"].as_str() != Some(expected_subject)\n+            || details[\"expires_at\"]\n+                .as_i64()\n+                .is_none_or(|expiry| expiry <= now)\n+            || !valid_user_id(user_id)\n+            || version.trim().is_empty()\n+        {\n+            return Err(AclError::Unauthorized);\n+        }\n+        Ok(Self {\n+            user_id: user_id.to_owned(),\n+            is_admin,\n+            authorization_version: version.to_owned(),\n+        })\n+    }\n+\n+    pub fn user_id(&self) -> &str {\n+        &self.user_id\n+    }\n+    pub fn is_admin(&self) -> bool {\n+        self.is_admin\n+    }\n+    pub fn authorization_version(&self) -> &str {\n+        &self.authorization_version\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+/// Fresh verification for one synchronous ACL operation. Never cache across\n+/// requests. The caller owns the revocation barrier before upstream side effects.\n+pub struct RequestIdentity(Identity);\n+\n+impl RequestIdentity {\n+    pub fn verify(\n+        pinned: &Identity,\n+        details: &Value,\n+        expected_subject: &str,\n+        now: i64,\n+    ) -> Result<Self> {\n+        let current = Identity::from_authority(details, expected_subject, now)?;\n+        if &current != pinned {\n+            return Err(AclError::Unauthorized);\n+        }\n+        Ok(Self(current))\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+fn valid_user_id(value: &str) -> bool {\n+    value\n+        .as_bytes()\n+        .first()\n+        .is_some_and(|c| (b'1'..=b'9').contains(c))\n+        && value.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit())\n+}\n+\n+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n+pub Self::Task => \"task\",\n+            Self::Connector => \"connector\",\n+        }\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]\n+pub struct ResourceKey {\n+    account_id: String,\n+     kind,\n+            upstream_id: upstream_id.into(),\n+        })\n+    }\n+    pub fn account_id(&self) -> &str {\n+        &self.account_id\n+    }\n+    pub fn kind(&self) -> ResourceKind {\n+ .transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;\n+        require_known(&tx, resource)?;\n+        if grant {\n+            tx.execute(\"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO
